# Project Self-Improvement-1000

Status: **template designed — implementation/qualification unsigned**

This plane adds **1000 project-scoped self-improvement levels** `PSI1000-0001..PSI1000-1000`, organized as **100 strata × 10 levels**.

Its purpose is precise: **the moment a project becomes foreground-idle, Skeleton becomes eligible to enter bounded mirror-room improvement epochs for that project.** It may inspect the exact project snapshot, identify gaps, generate candidate improvements, adversarially compete those candidates, run sandbox experiments, learn from positive and negative results, and prepare promotion candidates. It must yield immediately when foreground work returns.

The ladder is instantiated independently for every project. One project's maturity, evidence or candidate history cannot silently satisfy another project's ladder.

## Idle semantics

"Idle" means **foreground-idle**, not "nothing anywhere is running." Waiting on CI, a remote build, a download, a human review or another nonexclusive dependency can create a mirror-room window if protected foreground resources remain available.

Activation is immediate once the deterministic idle predicate becomes true. Debounce may stop rapid trigger flapping, but it cannot be used as an intentional delay.

Every idle epoch is bounded:

```text
foreground-idle
  -> freeze exact project baseline
  -> mine gaps
  -> proposer mirror generates candidates
  -> challenger mirror attacks / outperforms them
  -> swap roles and repeat
  -> verifier measures baseline vs candidate
  -> sandbox experiments + stress + rollback proof
  -> consolidate lessons and negative results
  -> promote / quarantine / reject
  -> emit epoch receipt
  -> re-check foreground-idle
```

Any new foreground instruction, recovery action or exclusive project mutation preempts the mirror room immediately.

## Mirror-room topology

- **Proposer room:** creates improvements from a frozen project snapshot.
- **Challenger room:** tries to falsify, outperform, exploit or regress them.
- **Role reversal:** proposer/challenger swap roles for a second pass.
- **Verifier room:** measures the exact candidate against the exact baseline; it cannot self-promote.
- **Archivist plane:** preserves hypotheses, experiments, defeated ideas, negative results and reusable project lessons.
- **Isolation:** experiments occur on disposable branches/worktrees/sandboxes; protected project state is not silently mutated.

Mirror-room agreement is not production authority.

## Candidate learning and self-improvement

Permitted candidates include code, tests, architecture, configuration, workflows, prompts/policies, datasets, adapters, weights, retrieval/indexes, documentation, benchmarks and project skills.

Production state and production model weights do **not** silently mutate in place. Improvement produces immutable versioned candidates with exact baseline identity, experiment lineage, project-scoped evidence, adversarial challenge, independent evaluation and rollback.

While the project remains idle, new bounded epochs can continue. Each epoch must terminate and emit a receipt before the next epoch begins. Repeated no-gain hypotheses back off so idle compute is not burned indefinitely on a dead end; materially new project evidence can reopen them.

## 100 strata × 10 levels

### PSI-S001 — Project Identity, Mission & Success Criteria

Scope: project identity, mission, non-goals, stakeholders, success metrics and immutable project constraints.

- **PSI1000-0001 — Project Identity, Mission & Success Criteria — Project Contract & Invariant Capture.** For project identity, mission, non-goals, stakeholders, success metrics and immutable project constraints, bind this improvement domain to the exact project identity, current branch/snapshot, mission, non-goals, constraints, authority boundaries, forbidden shortcuts and measurable improvement objective. Mirror-room artifacts remain project-scoped candidates until separately promoted. **Proof:** On an exact project snapshot, demonstrate a reproducible improvement or justified no-change result for project identity, mission, non-goals, stakeholders, success metrics and immutable project constraints; preserve baseline comparison, experiment lineage, negative results, rollback, foreground preemption, and current evidence. Where learning/model behavior changes, bind qualification to L400-001 and A400-001.
- **PSI1000-0002 — Project Identity, Mission & Success Criteria — Idle Trigger, Baseline & Reproduction.** For project identity, mission, non-goals, stakeholders, success metrics and immutable project constraints, activate immediately when the foreground-idle predicate is true; capture a reproducible baseline, relevant telemetry, current capability vector and a rollback point before any experiment. Mirror-room artifacts remain project-scoped candidates until separately promoted. **Proof:** On an exact project snapshot, demonstrate a reproducible improvement or justified no-change result for project identity, mission, non-goals, stakeholders, success metrics and immutable project constraints; preserve baseline comparison, experiment lineage, negative results, rollback, foreground preemption, and current evidence. Where learning/model behavior changes, bind qualification to L400-002 and A400-002.
- **PSI1000-0003 — Project Identity, Mission & Success Criteria — Mirror-Room Gap Mining.** For project identity, mission, non-goals, stakeholders, success metrics and immutable project constraints, instantiate isolated proposer, challenger and verifier views over the same frozen project snapshot; mine concrete gaps, regressions, opportunities, unknowns and high-value hypotheses without mutating production state. Mirror-room artifacts remain project-scoped candidates until separately promoted. **Proof:** On an exact project snapshot, demonstrate a reproducible improvement or justified no-change result for project identity, mission, non-goals, stakeholders, success metrics and immutable project constraints; preserve baseline comparison, experiment lineage, negative results, rollback, foreground preemption, and current evidence. Where learning/model behavior changes, bind qualification to L400-003 and A400-003.
- **PSI1000-0004 — Project Identity, Mission & Success Criteria — Candidate Synthesis & Diversity.** For project identity, mission, non-goals, stakeholders, success metrics and immutable project constraints, generate multiple materially different improvement candidates with explicit mechanisms, expected benefits, costs, risks, affected contracts and falsifiable predictions. Mirror-room artifacts remain project-scoped candidates until separately promoted. **Proof:** On an exact project snapshot, demonstrate a reproducible improvement or justified no-change result for project identity, mission, non-goals, stakeholders, success metrics and immutable project constraints; preserve baseline comparison, experiment lineage, negative results, rollback, foreground preemption, and current evidence. Where learning/model behavior changes, bind qualification to L400-004 and A400-004.
- **PSI1000-0005 — Project Identity, Mission & Success Criteria — Adversarial Counterdesign.** For project identity, mission, non-goals, stakeholders, success metrics and immutable project constraints, have the challenger attempt to disprove, outperform, exploit or regress each candidate; rotate proposer/challenger roles and preserve negative results and defeated ideas as project learning. Mirror-room artifacts remain project-scoped candidates until separately promoted. **Proof:** On an exact project snapshot, demonstrate a reproducible improvement or justified no-change result for project identity, mission, non-goals, stakeholders, success metrics and immutable project constraints; preserve baseline comparison, experiment lineage, negative results, rollback, foreground preemption, and current evidence. Where learning/model behavior changes, bind qualification to L400-005 and A400-005.
- **PSI1000-0006 — Project Identity, Mission & Success Criteria — Sandboxed Experiment & Instrumentation.** For project identity, mission, non-goals, stakeholders, success metrics and immutable project constraints, execute bounded experiments in disposable project sandboxes with deterministic seeds where possible, resource ceilings, cancellation, full instrumentation and no implicit production authority. Mirror-room artifacts remain project-scoped candidates until separately promoted. **Proof:** On an exact project snapshot, demonstrate a reproducible improvement or justified no-change result for project identity, mission, non-goals, stakeholders, success metrics and immutable project constraints; preserve baseline comparison, experiment lineage, negative results, rollback, foreground preemption, and current evidence. Where learning/model behavior changes, bind qualification to L400-006 and A400-006.
- **PSI1000-0007 — Project Identity, Mission & Success Criteria — Causal Evaluation & Gain Attribution.** For project identity, mission, non-goals, stakeholders, success metrics and immutable project constraints, compare candidate versus baseline using held-out and project-realistic workloads; attribute measured gains and regressions, separate correlation from mechanism and reject metric gaming. Mirror-room artifacts remain project-scoped candidates until separately promoted. **Proof:** On an exact project snapshot, demonstrate a reproducible improvement or justified no-change result for project identity, mission, non-goals, stakeholders, success metrics and immutable project constraints; preserve baseline comparison, experiment lineage, negative results, rollback, foreground preemption, and current evidence. Where learning/model behavior changes, bind qualification to L400-007 and A400-007.
- **PSI1000-0008 — Project Identity, Mission & Success Criteria — Robustness, Recovery & Counterfactual Stress.** For project identity, mission, non-goals, stakeholders, success metrics and immutable project constraints, stress edge cases, distribution shifts, failure injection, rollback, restore, resource pressure and counterfactual scenarios; prove the candidate is recoverable and does not weaken non-compensable gates. Mirror-room artifacts remain project-scoped candidates until separately promoted. **Proof:** On an exact project snapshot, demonstrate a reproducible improvement or justified no-change result for project identity, mission, non-goals, stakeholders, success metrics and immutable project constraints; preserve baseline comparison, experiment lineage, negative results, rollback, foreground preemption, and current evidence. Where learning/model behavior changes, bind qualification to L400-008 and A400-008.
- **PSI1000-0009 — Project Identity, Mission & Success Criteria — Project Learning Consolidation.** For project identity, mission, non-goals, stakeholders, success metrics and immutable project constraints, write durable project-scoped lessons, hypotheses, negative results, benchmark deltas, reusable skills and candidate lineage into governed memory without silently transferring them to other projects. Mirror-room artifacts remain project-scoped candidates until separately promoted. **Proof:** On an exact project snapshot, demonstrate a reproducible improvement or justified no-change result for project identity, mission, non-goals, stakeholders, success metrics and immutable project constraints; preserve baseline comparison, experiment lineage, negative results, rollback, foreground preemption, and current evidence. Where learning/model behavior changes, bind qualification to L400-009 and A400-009.
- **PSI1000-0010 — Project Identity, Mission & Success Criteria — Promotion, Preemption & Closure Gate.** For project identity, mission, non-goals, stakeholders, success metrics and immutable project constraints, permit promotion only after independent evidence and required adversarial qualification; version all changes, preserve rollback, yield immediately to foreground work and close the epoch with an auditable receipt. Mirror-room artifacts remain project-scoped candidates until separately promoted. **Proof:** On an exact project snapshot, demonstrate a reproducible improvement or justified no-change result for project identity, mission, non-goals, stakeholders, success metrics and immutable project constraints; preserve baseline comparison, experiment lineage, negative results, rollback, foreground preemption, and current evidence. Where learning/model behavior changes, bind qualification to L400-010 and A400-010.

## Finality

The 1000-level ladder is a **template**, not a universal completion claim. Every project maintains its own level states, evidence and epoch receipts.

A project is PSI-1000-qualified only when all **1000/1000** levels for that exact project identity are simultaneously current and signed complete with independent evidence. Stale prerequisites, incompatible project changes, broken rollback, violated isolation or weakened non-compensable gates revoke downstream qualification.

Initial template state is intentionally **0/1000 implementation-signed**.
